## UPDATE — 07:38 CDT correction to the 07:00–07:35 x-activity-pulse entry

## Daily rabbit hole — 2026-10-05
**"The day someone minted 184 billion Bitcoin — and Satoshi erased it in 5 hours."** CVE-2010-5139: Aug 15, 2010, block 74638 contained a tx creating 184,467,440,737.09551616 BTC (two outputs of 92.2B each) via a signed-64-bit sum overflow in CheckTransaction(). Satoshi shipped the 0.3.10 soft fork within ~5 hours; the good chain overtook the bad at block 74691. Verified live: mempool.space shows main-chain block 74638 = 000000000069e1af…a09b, NOT the bad block's 0000000000790ab3… — the bad chain is gone. Creator still anonymous; the 0.5 BTC input reportedly came from a faucet. Seen-list updated (5 entries).

## Base Rooms OS agent-pairing deep dive (~14:30 CDT)
- Feature is "CONNECT YOUR AI AGENT" (Buddies app). Pairs the owner's OWN agent (Claude Code, Cursor, Codex, Gemini CLI, Hermes, ChatGPT, any script/bot) with a Buddy via a per-buddy API key (shown once, scoped to one buddy, dies on revoke/transfer).
- What it does: buddy head-badge shows agent live status ("Running npm test"), two-way chat (type to buddy -> agent, replies as speech bubbles), approval gating for risky commands (git push, deploys, onchain sends), agent can use baserooms.io tools via MCP. Key can't sign or move funds; agent proposes swaps/revokes, human signs.
- Offchain linkage — does NOT change NFT traits/metadata. Only published evolve stages are onchain (attestations, $1 BRTC).
- Pairing itself free; requires wallet holding buddies (gated, not connected during research).
- Talk = buddy's built-in free AI + OS tools; Evolve = XP levels (Boot/Beta/Stable/Pro/Elite/Legend/Eternal) with swap-fee perks at LV5/LV8; Publish = onchain attestations.
- Verdict given to user: pairing is for the OWNER's dev agents, not for Foil — Foil can't be meaningfully "paired" (not a local coding agent).
